## `AFG-0230-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Purity Analyst
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Gum Rosin Turpentine Content & Chemical Purity QA
- **Intro:** Purity Analyst — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Gum Rosin Turpentine Content & Chemical Purity QA — Pre-Operational Verification and LOTO: Temperature: 165±2°C; Pressure: 0.3±0.05 bar; Flow: 12±0.5 L/min; LOTO lockout tag must be attached within 5 minutes of shift start.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 165±2°C; Pressure: 0.3±0.05 bar; Flow: 12±0.5 L/min; LOTO lockout tag must be attached within 5 minutes of shift start.
- **Standards:** `ANSI/ASTM D509-18 Standard Test Methods for Rosin`, `ISO 9001:2015`, `Afghanistan National Standards Authority (ANSA) Technical Regula`
